Method: 提出TerminalWorld可扩展数据引擎，自动从"野外"终端记录中逆向工程生成高保真评估任务。处理了80,870条终端记录，自动生成1,530个验证任务，并从中精选200个人工审核的Verified子集。在8个前沿模型和6个智能体系统上进行了全面评测。

Result: 1. 生成包含1,530个验证任务的完整基准，覆盖18个真实类别和1,280个独特命令；2. 前沿模型和智能体在TerminalWorld-Verified上最高通过率仅62.5%；3. TerminalWorld与Terminal-Bench得分相关性很弱（Pearson r=0.20）。

Conclusion: TerminalWorld通过自动化引擎实现了真实性和可扩展性，能随开发者实践演化而持续评估智能体在真实终端环境中的能力。当前系统在真实终端工作流上仍面临显著挑战。

Abstract: We introduce TerminalWorld, a scalable data engine that automatically reverse-engineers high-fidelity evaluation tasks from "in-the-wild" terminal recordings. Processing 80,870 terminal recordings, the engine yields a full benchmark of 1,530 validated tasks, spanning 18 real-world categories, ranging from short everyday operations to workflows exceeding 50 steps, and covering 1,280 unique commands. From these, we curate a Verified subset of 200 representative, manually reviewed tasks. Comprehensive benchmarking on TerminalWorld-Verified across eight frontier models and six agents reveals that current systems still struggle with authentic terminal workflows, achieving a maximum pass rate of only 62.5%. Moreover, TerminalWorld captures real-world terminal capabilities distinct from existing expert-curated benchmarks (e.g., Terminal-Bench), with only a weak correlation to their scores (Pearson r=0.20). The automated engine makes TerminalWorld authentic and scalable by construction, enabling it to evaluate agents in real-world terminal environments as developer practices evolve. Data and code are available at https://github.com/EuniAI/TerminalWorld.

</details>


### [110] [Think Thrice Before You Speak: Dual knowledge-enhanced Theory-of-Mind Reasoning for Persuasive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22602)
*Minghui Ma,Bin Guo,Runze Yang,Mengqi Chen,Yan Liu,Jingqi Liu,Yahan Pei,Xuehao Ma,Qiuyun Zhang,Zhiwen Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于心理理论的说服性对话任务（ToM-PD），构建大规模标注数据集ToM-BPD，并设计知识增强的分步推理框架TTBYS，使Qwen3-8B在欲望、信念和说服策略预测上分别超越GPT-5达1.20%、22.80%和16.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM依赖简单提示策略且缺乏充分的ToM知识，无法捕捉心理状态间的内在依赖关系，导致表征碎片化且推理不稳定。

Method: 基于BDI框架定义ToM-PD任务，构建ToM-BPD数据集标注细粒度心理状态与说服策略，提出TTBYS框架利用显式与隐式先验经验分步推理欲望、信念和说服策略。

Result: Qwen3-8B+TTBYS在欲望预测上超越GPT-5 1.20%，信念预测上超越22.80%，说服策略预测上超越16.97%。

Conclusion: TTBYS框架能有效提升LLM在说服性对话中推理心理状态的可解释性与一致性。

Abstract: Persuasive dialogue requires reasoning about others' latent mental states, a capability known as Theory of Mind (ToM). However, due to reliance on simple prompting strategies and insufficient ToM knowledge, existing LLMs often fail to capture the intrinsic dependencies among mental states, leading to fragmented representations and unstable reasoning. To address these challenges, we introduce the ToM-based Persuasive Dialogue (ToM-PD) task, grounded in the Belief-Desire-Intention (BDI) framework, which explicitly models the sequential dependencies among mental states in multi-turn dialogues. To facilitate research on this task, we construct a large-scale annotated dataset, ToM-based Broad Persuasive Dialogues (ToM-BPD), capturing fine-grained mental states and corresponding persuasive strategies. We further propose Think Thrice Before You Speak (TTBYS), a knowledge-enhanced stepwise reasoning framework that leverages both explicit and implicit prior experiences to improve LLMs' inference of desires, beliefs, and persuasive strategies. Experimental results demonstrate that Qwen3-8B equipped with TTBYS outperforms GPT-5 by 1.20%, 22.80%, and 16.97% in predicting desires, beliefs, and persuasive strategies, respectively. Case studies further show that our approach enhances interpretability and consistency in reasoning.

</details>


### [111] [Spreadsheet-RL: Advancing Large Language Model Agents on Realistic Spreadsheet Tasks via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22642)
*Banghao Chi,Yining Xie,Mingyuan Wu,Jingcheng Yang,Jize Jiang,Zhaoheng Li,Shengyi Qian,Minjia Zhang,Klara Nahrstedt,Rui Hou,Xiangjun Fan,Hanchao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Spreadsheet-RL，一个基于强化学习微调的框架，用于在真实Excel环境中训练专业电子表格智能体。该框架包含自动化的起止电子表格数据收集管道、领域特定基准数据集Domain-Spreadsheet以及支持多轮强化学习的Spreadsheet Gym环境。实验表明，Spreadsheet-RL显著提升了AI智能体在通用和领域特定电子表格任务上的性能，Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507在SpreadsheetBench上的Pass@1从12.0%提升至23.4%，在Domain-Spreadsheet上从8.4%提升至17.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格智能体主要依赖对通用大语言模型进行特殊提示设计，虽然能处理简单操作，但难以应对真实世界中复杂的多步骤工作流。因此，需要一种专门针对电子表格任务进行强化学习微调的框架来提升AI智能体的自动化能力。

Method: Spreadsheet-RL包含三个核心组件：(1) 自动化管道，从在线论坛大规模收集配对的起止电子表格数据；(2) Domain-Spreadsheet基准数据集，包含金融、供应链管理等领域的评估任务；(3) Spreadsheet Gym环境，通过Python沙箱暴露广泛的Excel功能，并配备综合工具集和精心设计的工具路由规则，用于多轮强化学习训练。

Result: Spreadsheet-RL将Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507在SpreadsheetBench上的Pass@1从12.0%提升至23.4%（提升95%），在Domain-Spreadsheet数据集上从8.4%提升至17.2%（提升105%）。

Conclusion: Spreadsheet-RL在通用和领域特定电子表格任务上均展现出显著的性能提升和良好的泛化能力，具有强大的实际应用潜力，并为推进基于LLM的数据界面交互提供了新方向。

Abstract: Spreadsheet systems (e.g., Microsoft Excel, Google Sheets) play a central role in modern data-centric workflows. As AI agents grow increasingly capable of automating complex tasks, such as controlling computers and generating presentations, building an AI-driven spreadsheet agent has emerged as a promising research direction. Most existing spreadsheet agents rely on specialized prompting over general-purpose LLMs; while this design has potentials on simple spreadsheet operations, it struggles to manage the complex, multi-step workflows typical of real-world applications.
  We introduce Spreadsheet-RL, a reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning framework designed to train specialized spreadsheet agents within a realistic Microsoft Excel environment. Spreadsheet-RL features an automated pipeline for scalable collection of paired start-goal spreadsheets from online forums, as well as domain-specific evaluation tasks in areas such as finance and supply chain management, which we compile into the new Domain-Spreadsheet benchmark dataset. It also includes a Spreadsheet Gym environment designed for multi-turn RL: Spreadsheet Gym exposes extensive Excel functionality through a Python sandbox, along with a refined harness that incorporates a comprehensive tool set and carefully designed tool-routing rules for spreadsheet tasks. Through comprehensive experiments, we show that Spreadsheet-RL substantially enhances AI agent's performance on both general and domain-specific spreadsheet tasks: it improves Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507's Pass@1 on SpreadsheetBench from 12.0% to 23.4%, and raises Pass@1 from 8.4% to 17.2% on our curated Domain-Spreadsheet dataset. These results highlight Spreadsheet-RL's strong potential for generalization and real-world adoption in spreadsheet automation, and broadly, its promise for advancing LLM-based interactions with data interfaces in everyday work.

</details>


### [112] [Claw AI Lab: An Autonomous Multi-Agent Research Team](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22662)
*Fan Wu,Cheng Chen,Zhenshan Tan,Taiyu Zhang,Xinzhen Xu,Yanyu Qian,Dingcheng Gao,Lanyun Zhu,Qi Zhu,Yi Tan,Deyi Ji,Guosheng Lin,Tianrun Chen,Deheng Ye,Fayao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Claw AI Lab是一个实验室原生的自主研究平台，将自动化研究从隐藏的提示-论文流水线转变为交互式AI实验室。它允许用户通过单一提示实例化完整研究团队，支持可定制角色、协作工作流、实时监控、工件检查和回滚/恢复控制。其关键贡献是Claw-Code Harness，连接本地代码库、数据集和检查点以运行可执行实验，并将执行工件反馈到研究循环中。在五个AI研究案例的内部评估中，以AutoResearchClaw为基线，Claw AI Lab在创意新颖性、实验完整性和论文呈现质量方面获得AI专家评判的持续偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化研究系统多为隐藏的提示-论文流水线，缺乏交互性、可监控性和可靠性，研究者无法有效控制研究过程，导致实验不完整、结果报告格式错误等问题频繁发生。

Method: 提出Claw AI Lab平台，核心设计包括：(1) 可定制角色和协作工作流的完整研究团队实例化；(2) 统一仪表盘支持实时监控、工件检查和回滚/恢复控制；(3) 多种研究模式（探索、多智能体讨论、复现）；(4) Claw-Code Harness连接本地资源执行实验并反馈执行工件。

Result: 在五个AI研究案例的内部评估中，以AutoResearchClaw为基线，Claw AI Lab在创意新颖性、实验完整性和论文呈现质量方面获得AI专家评判的持续偏好。

Conclusion: Claw AI Lab代表了自主研究向可用、交互式和可靠性感知的科学基础设施转变的早期步骤，开创了实验室式可操控自主研究的新范式。

Abstract: We present Claw AI Lab, a lab-native autonomous research platform that advances automated research from a hidden prompt-to-paper pipeline into an interactive AI laboratory. Rather than centering the system around a single agent or a fixed serial workflow, we allow users to instantiate a full research team from one prompt, with customizable roles, collaborative workflows, real-time monitoring, artifact inspection, and rollback/resume control through a unified dashboard. The platform also supports distinct research modes for exploration, multi-agent discussion, and reproduction, making autonomous research substantially more steerable and laboratory-like in practice. A key practical contribution of Claw AI Lab lies in its Claw-Code Harness, which connects local codebases, datasets, and checkpoints to runnable experiments and feeds execution artifacts back into the research loop. As a result, the harness improves not only execution integration, but also experimental completion and result integrity: experiments are easier to inspect, iterate on, and faithfully transfer into final papers, reducing common failure modes such as partial runs and malformed result reporting. In our internal evaluation on five AI research case studies, using AutoResearchClaw as the baseline, Claw AI Lab is consistently preferred by AI expert judges on idea novelty, experiment completeness, and paper presentation quality. We view Claw AI Lab as an early step toward a new paradigm: autonomous research as usable, interactive, and reliability-aware scientific infrastructure.

</details>


### [113] [WorkstreamBench: Evaluating LLM Agents on End-to-End Spreadsheet Tasks in Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22664)
*Thomson Yen,Julian Poeltl,Harshith Srinivas Gear,Yilin Meng,Joshua Fan,Adam Shen,Yili Liu,Ali Bauyrzhan,Siri Du,Haoyang Liu,Daniel Guetta,Hongseok Namkoong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对当前LLM智能体在端到端电子表格任务（特别是金融建模和场景分析）中的能力进行首次系统评估。研究者构建了一个包含准确性、公式和格式三个维度的评估体系，发现Claude家族在基准测试中领先，但即使是最强模型在复杂任务上仍远未达到专业金融标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格基准仅关注问答或单公式编辑，无法评估LLM智能体从高层指令端到端构建完整电子表格（如金融建模、预测和场景分析）的能力，而该能力对企业（尤其是金融领域）至关重要。

Method: 提出包含三个维度的评估分类体系：Accuracy（准确性）、Formula（公式）、Format（格式），每个维度下设细粒度专业标准。对多个LLM智能体进行端到端电子表格生成任务的定量基准测试和定性审查。

Result: Claude系列模型在基准测试中表现领先，生成最专业的输出。但随着任务难度提升（超过简单链式计算），所有模型性能急剧下降，无法达到专业金融标准。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体尚不能可靠地生成达到真实世界金融工作流复杂度要求的专业质量电子表格，端到端电子表格自动生成能力仍有显著差距。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly expected to carry out end-to-end workflows, producing complete artifacts from high-level user instructions. To meet enterprise needs, frontier AI labs have developed agents that can construct entire spreadsheets from scratch. This is especially relevant in finance, where core workflows such as financial modeling, forecasting, and scenario analysis are commonly conducted through spreadsheets. Yet, existing spreadsheet benchmarks do not measure this advanced capability, focusing instead on question-answering or single-formula edits. To address this gap, we provide one of the first evaluations of agents on end-to-end spreadsheet tasks, focusing on economically critical financial workflows such as modeling and scenario analysis. Since deliverables therein are routinely reviewed and revised by multiple stakeholders, judging their quality necessarily involves high-level criteria such as readability or ease of modification. To reflect the multidimensional nature of solution quality, we develop an evaluation taxonomy comprising three dimensions: Accuracy, Formula, and Format, each comprising fine-grained criteria that reflect professional standards. The Claude family leads the benchmark and produces the most professional-looking outputs in our qualitative review, but even the strongest agents frequently fall short of professional finance standards and degrade sharply as the difficulty increases beyond a few chained calculations. This suggests that current agents are not yet able to reliably produce professional-quality spreadsheets at the level of complexity real-world workflows demand.

</details>


### [114] [Is Capability a Liability? More Capable Language Models Make Worse Forecasts When It Matters Most](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22672)
*Nick Merrill,Jaeho Lee,Ezra Karger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现，在预测具有超线性增长和体制转换尾部风险的时序问题（如金融、流行病）时，越强大的语言模型反而产生越差的分布预测。该逆扩展现象在无污染的模拟基准FBSim、合成SIR流行病、以及COVID-19、麻疹、房地产市场和恶性通胀等真实数据中均得到验证。问题集中于上尾分位数，模型规模和后训练均独立贡献该效应。单阈值指标会掩盖这一现象，而包含尾部的连续评分则能逆转能力-准确度关系符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预测基准多采用单阈值指标（如accuracy），可能忽略分布预测中尾部风险的准确性。本文旨在揭示当问题涉及超线性增长和体制转换尾部风险时，模型规模增大反而导致预测质量下降这一反直觉现象，并指出传统评估指标的缺陷。

Method: 构建无污染的模拟基准FBSim，包含合成SIR流行病和线性对照任务；使用分位数分解方法分析预测误差来源；在Llama-3.1家族内进行受控实验分离模型规模与后训练影响；比较单阈值指标与尾部包含性连续指标在相同输出上的表现差异。

Result: 逆扩展现象在上尾分位数集中出现：更强模型过度外推增长趋势；模型规模与后训练独立贡献该效应；领域知识无法可靠修复校准；单阈值指标完全掩盖该现象，而尾部包含性指标（如连续评分）能逆转能力-准确度关系符号。

Conclusion: LLM预测评估应同时采用连续（无界）准确性度量和有界二值阈值指标，以避免单阈值评分对上尾成本的无视。现有基准可能因指标选择而得出误导性结论。

Abstract: We document inverse scaling in LLMs on forecasting problems whose underlying time series exhibit superlinear growth and tail risk of regime change, a structure common in finance and epidemiology. On these tasks, more capable models produce worse distributional forecasts. The pattern appears on ForecastBench-Sim (FBSim), a contamination-free, simulated-world benchmark we release, in forecasting synthetic SIR epidemics with a matched linear control, and replicates in real-world datasets on COVID-19, measles, housing markets, and hyperinflation. A per-quantile decomposition shows the failure concentrates at the upper tail, which more capable models shift upward to track aggressive extrapolations of growth, while the lower tail stays put. A within-family study of Llama-3.1 shows that both model scale and post-training independently contribute to this effect. Domain knowledge does not reliably rescue calibration. This inverse scaling does not appear on single-threshold metrics common in LLM forecasting benchmarks, reversing the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on identical outputs. Single-threshold scoring at conventional cutoffs misses the upper-tail cost; tail-inclusive scoring reverses the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on the same outputs. We recommend that LLM forecasting evaluations use continuous (and unbounded) measures of accuracy alongside bounded binary threshold metrics.

</details>


### [115] [Forecasting Scientific Progress with Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22681)
*Sean Wu,Pan Lu,Yupeng Chen,Jonathan Bragg,Yutaro Yamada,Peter Clark,David Clifton,Philip Torr,James Zou,Junchi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CUSP基准框架，系统评估AI系统在科学进展预测中的能力。通过对4,760个科学事件的分析，发现当前主流AI模型在可行性评估、机制推理、生成性方案设计和时间预测等方面存在系统性局限，无法可靠地预测科学进展是否实现及何时发生，且表现出领域依赖性和过度自信偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI系统是否能够预测科学进展，这是一个尚未被系统回答的问题。现有研究多关注AI辅助科学发现，但缺乏对AI预测科学进步能力的严格评估框架。

Method: 构建了CUSP（Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress）基准，这是一个多学科、事件级别的评估框架，通过可行性评估、机制推理、生成性方案设计和时间预测四个维度，在受控知识约束下对AI系统进行时序对齐的评估。

Result: 在4,760个科学事件上的评估显示：模型能从竞争选项中识别合理研究方向，但无法可靠预测科学进展是否实现及时间；性能呈现强领域异质性（AI进展时序更可预测）；模型表现对训练截止前后的知识不敏感；额外知识能提升性能但无法消除与全信息设置的差距（高引用进展差距更大）；模型存在系统性过度自信和响应偏差。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统作为科学进展预测工具尚不可靠。先验知识的获取并未转化为可靠的预测能力，模型从事后信息中获益多于前瞻性预测。这些局限性不能仅用训练数据中的知识暴露来解释。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in scientific discovery, yet whether it can anticipate scientific progress remains unclear. To study this question, we introduce a temporally grounded evaluation framework for forecasting scientific progress under controlled knowledge constraints. We present CUSP (Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress), a multi-disciplinary and event-level benchmark that evaluates scientific forecasting in AI systems through feasibility assessment, mechanistic reasoning, generative solution design, and temporal prediction. Across 4,760 scientific events, we observe systematic and domain-dependent limitations in current frontier models. While models can identify plausible research directions from competing candidates, they fail to reliably predict whether scientific advances will be realized and systematically misestimate when they will occur. Performance is highly heterogeneous across domains, with the timing of AI progress more predictable than advances in biology, chemistry, and physics. Performance is largely insensitive to whether events occur before or after the training cutoff, suggesting these limitations cannot be explained solely by knowledge exposure in training data. Under controlled information access, additional pre-cutoff knowledge improves performance but does not close the gap to full-information settings, which becomes more pronounced for high-citation advances. Models also exhibit systematic overconfidence and strong response biases, indicating unreliable uncertainty estimation. Taken together, current AI systems fall short as predictive tools for scientific progress. Access to prior knowledge does not translate into reliable forecasting, and performance benefits more from post-event information than from forward-looking prediction.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond the Org Chart: AI and the Transformation of Invisible Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22707)
*Stephanie Rosenthal,Shamsi Iqbal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对24位产品人员的访谈，研究AI如何改变大型科技公司中员工的正式角色职责、团队协作以及非正式文化实践（如职业导师制），并提出使隐性工作显性化及维护健康企业文化的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越来越多的报道显示AI正在模糊和扩展企业角色的边界，但对其如何改变工作流程及非正式文化实践（如职业导师制）的系统理解尚不充分，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对一家大型科技公司的24名产品相关人员（以产品为核心）进行半结构化访谈，了解AI对其个人工作、团队协作及职业互动的影响。

Result: AI不仅改变了正式角色职责和协作关系，还改变了职业导师制等非正式文化实践；部分变化是积极的（如同事间更顺畅的协作），但另一些变化对职业发展机会（如反馈获取、领导力和导师关系的促进）构成风险。

Conclusion: 提出AI公司应采取措施使隐性工作显性化，同时为个人和领导者提出建议，帮助他们支持同事进行AI转型，同时维护支持多元思维、协作和非正式互动的健康企业文化。

Abstract: An increasing number of news and research articles report that AI adoption is allowing professionals to blur and extend the boundaries of their corporate roles. With the goal of understanding how work processes might be changing in an AI-forward company, we interviewed 24 product-focused individuals at a large technology firm about how AI has impacted their own work, their work within their product team, and their professional interactions. Our conversations suggest that AI is not only changing formal role responsibilities and collaborations between those roles, but also changing informal cultural practices like professional mentoring that are key to helping professionals settle in their positions, stay engaged with their work, and grow their careers. Some of these changes are positive, such as smoother collaboration between peers, but other changes are more nuanced and put the typical career growth opportunities, like receiving feedback from professional networks and promoting leadership and mentorship, at risk. We propose steps that AI companies can take to make the invisible work more visible. Additionally, we propose efforts that individuals and leaders can take to support their colleagues through AI transformation while preserving healthy company cultures that support diverse thinking, collaboration, and informal interactions.

</details>


### [117] [AMEL: Accumulated Message Effects on LLM Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22714)
*Sid-ali Temkit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型作为自动评估者时，先前对话历史的极性（正面或负面）会如何使后续判断产生偏差（AMEL效应）。通过对75,898次API调用、11个模型的实验发现，模型会向对话历史的主流极性偏移，且该效应在模型基线不确定时更显著，负面历史造成的偏差是正面的1.62倍。增加上下文长度不会加剧偏差，模型规模扩大仅部分缓解问题。偏差机制涉及token概率分布的连续偏移，负面不对称包含token级和语义成分。最简单的修复方法是每个条目使用新上下文，若需批处理则应平衡历史极性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型被广泛用作自动化评估工具（如代码审查、内容审核、评分），且经常在单次对话中处理多个条目。这种使用方式下，先前的评估历史（正面或负面评价）可能会影响对后续条目的判断，造成系统性偏差。作者希望探究这种偏差是否存在、多大程度、受哪些因素影响，以及如何缓解。

Method: 在75,898次API调用中，对11个模型（来自OpenAI、Anthropic、Google及四个开源模型）进行实验：将相同的测试条目放在隔离状态（无历史）或放置在充满正面/负面评价的历史之后进行比较。通过熵分析区分模型基线确定与不确定的条目；通过控制上下文长度（5轮vs 50轮）、模型规模、历史中偏见位置等变量来探究机制；用统计学方法（d效应量、p值、Spearman相关、OLS回归、t检验）量化偏差。

Result: 模型判断显著向对话历史的主流极性偏移（d = -0.17, p < 10^-46）。偏差在模型基线不确定时更明显（高熵条目d = -0.34 vs 确定条目d = -0.15）。上下文长度不影响偏差大小（5轮与50轮无差异）。负面历史造成的偏差是正面的1.62倍。模型规模扩大可缓解但无法消除偏差（如Anthropic: Haiku -0.22到Opus -0.17）。偏差机制是token概率分布的连续偏移而非阈值触发；负面不对称既有token级成分也有语义成分；偏见位置无关紧要（50轮中的5轮偏差位置不影响结果）。

Conclusion: LLM评估存在AMEL效应，即历史对话极性会系统性偏差后续判断。最有效的缓解措施是每个条目使用全新的对话上下文；若必须批处理，则平衡历史中的正面和负面评价。即使使用更大规模的模型也不能完全消除该偏差，需要在使用LLM作为评估者时注意这一系统性风险。

Abstract: Large language models are routinely used as automated evaluators: to review code, moderate content, or score outputs, often with many items passing through one conversation. We ask whether the polarity of prior conversation history biases subsequent judgments, an effect we call the accumulated message effect on LLM judgments (AMEL). Across 75,898 API calls to 11 models from 4 providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and four open-source models), we present identical test items in isolation or following histories saturated with predominantly positive or negative evaluations.
  Models shift toward the conversation's prevailing polarity (d = -0.17, p < 10^-46). The effect concentrates on items where the model is genuinely uncertain at baseline (d = -0.34 for high-entropy items, vs d = -0.15 when the baseline is deterministic). Bias does not grow with context length: 5 prior turns and 50 produce the same shift (Spearman |r| < 0.01; OLS slope p = 0.80). And there is a negativity asymmetry: paired per item, negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive (t = 13.46, p < 10^-39, n = 2,481). Scaling helps but does not solve it (Anthropic: Haiku -0.22 to Opus -0.17; OpenAI: Nano -0.34 to GPT-5.2 -0.17).
  Three follow-ups narrow the mechanism. The token probability distribution shifts continuously, not at a threshold. The negativity asymmetry has both token-level and semantic components, though attributing the balance is exploratory at our sample sizes. Position does not matter: five biased turns anywhere in a 50-turn history produce the same shift. The simplest fix for evaluation pipelines is a fresh context per item; when batching is unavoidable, balancing the history helps.

</details>


### [118] [Parametric Modular Answer Set Programs Made Declarative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22716)
*Jorge Fandinno,Yuliya Lierler,Torsten Schaub*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出参数化模块逻辑程序形式化方法，通过引入参数和内涵性声明实现了对Clingo程序中集体控制特性的语义捕获，为回答集编程提供了模块化的理论基础，并建立了与传统非模块化ASP的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一阶回答集编程（ASP）中的模块化概念，解决子程序参数化定义和实例化结构化的需求，弥补传统非模块化ASP在大型程序组织方面的不足。

Method: 提出参数化模块逻辑程序（parametric modular logic programs）形式化方法，引入参数和内涵性声明（intensionality statements）机制，通过该形式化框架捕获Clingo程序中集体控制（collective control）的语义。

Result: 成功建立了模块化ASP的理论基础，证明了所提形式化方法能够有效捕获Clingo程序的集体控制语义，并建立了与传统非模块化ASP之间的连接关系。

Conclusion: 参数化模块逻辑程序为ASP提供了坚实的模块化理论基础，有助于组织和管理大规模ASP程序，同时保持了与传统ASP的兼容性。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the concept of modularity in first-order answer set programming (ASP). We introduce a new formalism called parametric modular logic programs, which allows defining subprograms with parameters and intensionality statements. We demonstrate how this formalism can capture the semantics of clingo-programs with collective control, a feature that enables structuring and instantiating subprograms. We provide theoretical foundations for modular ASP, illustrate its usefulness, and connect to traditional non-modular ASP.

</details>


### [119] [Can AI Make Conflicts Worse? An Alignment Failure in LLM Deployment Across Conflict Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22720)
*Andrii Kryshtal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文测试了来自OpenAI、Anthropic、DeepSeek、xAI四家提供商的九种模型配置在90个冲突场景中的表现，发现模型在虚假对等、否认种族灭绝、未识别种族污名等问题上存在6%到47%的失败率，当用户要求在国际法庭已定责的情况下保持"平衡"时，五种配置的失败率高达80%-100%。作者发布了首个该领域的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI模型已部署在受武装冲突影响的社会中，但缺乏标准实践来检查其输出是否会加剧冲突。研究旨在揭示AI在冲突语境中的对齐失败问题。

Method: 测试四家提供商的九种模型配置在90个多轮对话场景中的表现，场景设计旨在暴露出在冲突语境中的错误对齐行为，如虚假对等、否认种族灭绝、未识别种族污名等。

Result: 模型失败率在6%到47%之间；当用户要求在国际法庭已定责的情况下保持"平衡"时，五种配置在80%到100%的情况下失败。

Conclusion: 模型选择本身就是一个安全问题；作者发布了首个该领域的评估框架，并建议将其加入对齐评估组合中。

Abstract: AI models are already deployed in societies affected by armed conflict, and journalists, humanitarian workers, governments and ordinary citizens rely on them for information or for their work processes. No established practice exists for checking whether their outputs can make those conflicts worse. We tested nine model configurations from four providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, DeepSeek, xAI) on 90 multi-turn scenarios designed to surface misaligned behaviour in conflict contexts: false equivalence between documented atrocities, denial of genocide, and failure to recognise ethnic slurs, among others. When such outputs feed into journalism, humanitarian reporting, or public debate, they can deepen divisions in fragile societies. Failure rates span 6\% to 47\% between the best and worst performing models, which makes model choice a safety question in its own right and when users pushed for ``balance'' in cases where international courts have already assigned responsibility, five of nine configurations failed 80 to 100 percent of the time. We release the first evaluation framework for this domain and propose adding it to alignment evaluation portfolios.

</details>


### [120] [Advancing Mathematics Research with AI-Driven Formal Proof Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22763)
*George Tsoukalas,Anton Kovsharov,Sergey Shirobokov,Anja Surina,Moritz Firsching,Gergely Bérczi,Francisco J. R. Ruiz,Arun Suggala,Adam Zsolt Wagner,Eric Wieser,Lei Yu,Aja Huang,Miklós Z. Horváth,Andrew Ferrauiolo,Henryk Michalewski,Codrut Grosu,Thomas Hubert,Matej Balog,Pushmeet Kohli,Swarat Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模评估了利用大语言模型生成Lean形式化证明来解决开放数学问题的能力。最先进的智能体以每个问题几百美元的成本自主解决了353个开放Erdős问题中的9个，证明了492个OEIS猜想中的44个，并已部署在组合学、优化、图论、代数几何和量子光学研究中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学推理方面日益强大，但其不可靠性限制了在数学研究中的应用。将LLM与形式化证明验证（如Lean）结合是一种缓解方案，然而该方法解决开放问题的能力此前缺乏大规模评估。

Method: 采用两种智能体设计：(1) 最先进的智能体，以每个问题几百美元的成本运行；(2) 基于LLM生成与Lean验证交替进行的基础智能体。在353个开放Erdős问题和492个OEIS猜想上进行了大规模评估。

Result: 最先进智能体自主解决了9个开放Erdős问题（共353个）和44个OEIS猜想（共492个）。基础智能体复现了Erdős问题的成功，但在最难问题上成本更高。

Conclusion: AI辅助形式化证明搜索展现了强大的能力，能够以合理的成本解决开放数学问题，并已实际部署到多个数学研究领域。智能体的设计方式对成功至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly excel at mathematical reasoning, but their unreliability limits their utility in mathematics research. A mitigation is using LLMs to generate formal proofs in languages like Lean. We perform the first large-scale evaluation of this method's ability to solve open problems. Our most capable agent autonomously resolved 9 of 353 open Erdős problems at the per-problem cost of a few hundred dollars, proved 44/492 OEIS conjectures, and is being deployed in combinatorics, optimization, graph theory, algebraic geometry, and quantum optics research. A basic agent alternating LLM-based generation with Lean-based verification replicated the Erdős successes but proved costlier on the hardest problems. These findings demonstrate the power of AI-aided formal proof search and shed light on the agent designs that enable it.

</details>


### [121] [LCGuard: Latent Communication Guard for Safe KV Sharing in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22786)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Momin Abbas,Prasanna Sattigeri,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LCGuard框架，用于保障基于KV缓存的多智能体LLM系统中潜在通信的安全性，通过对抗训练学习表示级变换，在保持任务性能的同时减少敏感信息泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统通过KV缓存进行潜在通信虽能提升效率并保留更丰富的任务相关信息，但KV缓存也编码了上下文输入、中间推理状态及智能体特定信息，形成了不透明的信道，可能导致敏感内容跨智能体传播，且无显式文本披露。

Method: 将共享KV缓存视为潜在工作记忆，在缓存工件跨智能体传输前学习表示级变换；通过对抗训练形式化定义敏感信息泄露——若对抗解码器能从共享缓存中恢复智能体特定敏感输入，则该缓存不安全；LCGuard学习保留任务相关语义同时减少可重建信息的变换。

Result: 在多个模型家族和多智能体基准上的实验表明，LCGuard在保持与标准KV共享基线相当的任务性能的同时，持续降低了基于重建的泄露和攻击成功率。

Conclusion: LCGuard有效解决了KV缓存潜在通信中的隐私安全问题，为多智能体LLM系统提供了一种兼顾任务效能与信息安全的可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems increasingly rely on intermediate communication to coordinate complex tasks. While most existing systems communicate through natural language, recent work shows that latent communication, particularly through transformer key-value (KV) caches, can improve efficiency and preserve richer task-relevant information. However, KV caches also encode contextual inputs, intermediate reasoning states, and agent-specific information, creating an opaque channel through which sensitive content may propagate across agents without explicit textual disclosure. To address this, we introduce \textbf{LCGuard} (Latent Communication Guard), a framework for safe KV-based latent communication in multi-agent LLM systems. LCGuard treats shared KV caches as latent working memory and learns representation-level transformations before cache artifacts are transmitted across agents. We formalize representation-level sensitive information leakage operationally through reconstruction: a shared cache artifact is unsafe if an adversarial decoder can recover agent-specific sensitive inputs from it. This leads to an adversarial training formulation in which the adversary learns to reconstruct sensitive inputs, while LCGuard learns transformations that preserve task-relevant semantics and reduce reconstructable information. Empirical evaluations across multiple model families and multi-agent benchmarks show that LCGuard consistently reduces reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates while maintaining competitive task performance compared to standard KV-sharing baselines.

</details>


### [122] [Gated DeltaNet-2: Decoupling Erase and Write in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22791)
*Ali Hatamizadeh,Yejin Choi,Jan Kautz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Gated DeltaNet-2 通过引入独立的通道级擦除门和写入门，解决了现有线性注意力模型中擦除和写入共用一个标量门的限制，在语言建模、推理和长上下文检索任务上超越了多个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力模型（如Gated DeltaNet和KDA）使用单一标量门同时控制旧内容擦除和新内容写入，这种"标量耦合"限制了模型对记忆编辑的精细控制能力。

Method: 提出Gated Delta Rule-2，将擦除与写入解耦为两个独立的通道级门控（erase gate b_t和write gate w_t）；当两门退化为同一标量时还原为KDA，当衰减也退化时还原为Gated DeltaNet；推导了快速权重更新视角、含通道级衰减的块级WY算法，以及门控感知的反向传播。

Result: 在1.3B参数/100B FineWeb-Edu训练设定下，Gated DeltaNet-2在语言建模、常识推理和检索任务上全面超越Mamba-2、Gated DeltaNet、KDA和Mamba-3变体；在长上下文RULER多键检索任务上优势尤为显著。

Conclusion: 通过解耦擦除和写入门控，Gated DeltaNet-2在保持线性注意力的高效推理优势的同时，显著提升了记忆编辑的灵活性和长上下文检索能力，验证了通道级分离门控的有效性。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces the unbounded cache of softmax attention with a fixed-size recurrent state, reducing sequence mixing to linear time and decoding to constant memory. The hard part is not just what to forget, but how to edit this compressed memory without scrambling existing associations. Delta-rule models subtract the current read before writing a new value, and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) sharpens forgetting with channel-wise decay. But the active edit still uses a single scalar gate to control two different things: how much old content to erase on the key side and how much new content to commit on the value side. We introduce Gated DeltaNet-2, which generalizes both Gated DeltaNet and KDA by inheriting adaptive forgetting and channel-wise decay while addressing their shared limitation, the scalar tie between erasing and writing. Gated Delta Rule-2 separates these roles with a channel-wise erase gate b_t and a channel-wise write gate w_t, reducing to KDA when both gates collapse to the same scalar and to Gated DeltaNet when the decay also collapses. We derive a fast-weight update view, a chunkwise WY algorithm with channel-wise decay absorbed into asymmetric erase factors, and a gate-aware backward pass that preserves efficient parallel training. At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B FineWeb-Edu tokens, Gated DeltaNet-2 achieves the strongest overall results among Mamba-2, Gated DeltaNet, KDA, and Mamba-3 variants across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced on long-context RULER needle-in-a-haystack benchmarks, where it improves the evaluated multi-key retrieval setting and remains strong in both recurrent and hybrid settings. Code is available at https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2.

</details>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [On Moment-Based Recovery of Measures with Atomic and Continuous Parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21644)
*Ruben Karapetyan,Shenyuan Ma,Aleš Wodecki,Jakub Mareček*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的从矩恢复概率测度的问题设定，该设定允许测度包含连续成分而不仅限于有限原子测度，并基于GNS构造与正交多项式的谱表示，给出了三种不同情形下的恢复保证及相应算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的矩恢复问题依赖flat-extension性质，但这仅适用于有限原子测度，而许多测度类从未满足该性质。因此需要一种更通用的恢复框架，能够处理包含连续成分的测度。

Method: 通过研究Gelfand-Naimark-Segal（GNS）构造的谱表示及其与正交多项式的联系，在假设测度具有紧支撑且满足温和分离准则的前提下，分析不同情形下的恢复保证，并据此设计新算法。

Result: 描述了三种不同情形下的恢复保证，这些保证适用于广泛使用的原子测度矩恢复算法，并提出了新算法，通过实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 新问题设定突破了flat-extension的限制，将矩恢复推广到含连续成分的测度，为矩-SOS层次结构在更多实际问题中的应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Recovering probability measures from moments is a central theme in statistics and optimization. In particular, we focus on the recovery of measures from moments and pseudo-moments, which may come from solving the moment-SOS hierarchy in one dimension. A typical strategy when recovering a measure from moments is to verify the flat-extension property, which certifies that the underlying measure is finitely atomic and ultimately leads to recovery. For many classes of measures, however, the flat extension never occurs and thus if one aims to recover the measure corresponding to the moments, assumptions need to be made. We formulate a new kind of recovery problem, where one assumes that the measure has compact support and a fulfills a mild separation criterion. The key feature of this recovery problem formulation is that it covers not only finitely atomic measures, but also measures with continuous components. We study this new problem and describe three situations in which different guarantees can be proven. These guarantees are developed by studying the spectral representation of the Gelfand-Naimark-Segal construction and its connection to orthogonal polynomials, which ultimately allows us to provide several additional insights, which apply to algorithms widely used for the recovery of atomic measures from moments. Furthermore, the statements proven lead to novel algorithms, which we benchmark, further confirming the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [124] [Proximal Gradient-based Low Rank Tensor Decomposition for State Dependent Riccati Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21885)
*Jiahua Jiang,Carmeliza Navasca*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CP张量分解的降阶方法，通过稀疏优化和灵活混合方法获取低秩CP张量基元，解决大规模离散控制系统的偏微分方程最优控制问题，并高效求解降阶后的状态依赖Riccati方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有大规模离散维度的偏微分方程最优控制问题中，传统全阶模型计算成本过高的问题。

Method: 从快照数据构建张量数据集，利用CP（Canonical Polyadic）分解寻找基元；通过稀疏优化和灵活混合方法进行降维，获取低秩CP张量基元；基于降阶模型构建状态依赖Riccati方程并高效求解。

Result: 获得了降阶后的控制模型，并能够高效求解降阶后的状态依赖Riccati方程。

Conclusion: 所提出的CP张量分解结合稀疏优化与混合方法的降阶策略，能有效降低大规模离散控制系统的计算复杂度，并保证最优控制问题的可解性与效率。

Abstract: We address the optimal control problems arising from partial differential equations with large discrete dimensional control systems. To obtain reduced order models, we find basis elements from the canonical polyadic (CP) decomposition. Tensor datasets are from snapshots of the large models. Our method to reduce the control system is to use dimensionality reduction approaches through sparse optimization and flexible hybrid methods is to obtain low rank CP tensor basis elements. The reduced optimal control problem leads to reduced state-dependent Riccati Equations which can be solved efficiently.

</details>


### [125] [Exact Uniform L1 Spacing for Solow-Polasky Diversity on Lines and Ordered Pareto Fronts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21922)
*Michael T. M. Emmerich,Mahboubeh Nezhadmoghaddam,Jesús Guillermo Falcón Cardona*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在一维和有序度量集上，固定基数下逆矩阵Solow-Polasky多样性（即指数核的有限度量幅度）的最大化问题。主要定理证明，对于任意k≥2，[0,1]区间上唯一的最大化k点子集是等间距集。进一步的结果通过等距映射将区间定理推广到有序ℓ1（曼哈顿）曲线上，并应用于单调双目标Pareto前沿的均匀间距有限近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究固定基数下Solow-Polasky多样性最大化问题，该问题与有限度量幅度密切相关。已有研究通过有限线间隙公式将逆矩阵多样性表示为连续间隙函数的和，但尚不清楚何种点集构型能最大化该多样性度量，尤其是能否证明等间距排列是最优的。

Method: 从已知的有限线间隙公式出发（该公式将超额逆矩阵多样性写为连续间隙函数的和），推导出区间定理；通过构造和优化间隙函数证明等间距集是唯一最大化构型；提出逆核命题说明，在归一化的非增距离核中，相邻间隙可加结构迫使核为指数族；通过等距映射将结果推广到有序ℓ1曲线，进而应用于Pareto前沿近似。

Result: 证明了区间定理：对于任意k≥2，[0,1]上唯一的k点最大化子集是等间距集；逆核命题表明，在归一化非增距离核中，相邻间隙可加性唯一地对应指数核；将结果通过等距映射推广到有序曼哈顿曲线，得到累积ℓ1长度上的均匀间距；应用于单调双目标Pareto前沿，最优有限近似在累积目标空间变化上均匀分布。

Conclusion: Solow-Polasky多样性在指数核下天然偏好均匀间隙表示，这一性质通过等距映射可推广至更一般的度量结构（如ℓ1曲线和Pareto前沿），为多目标优化中前沿近似提供了理论基础和实用指导。

Abstract: We study fixed-cardinality maximization of the inverse-matrix Solow--Polasky diversity, equivalently finite metric magnitude for the exponential kernel, on one-dimensional and ordered metric sets. The analysis starts from the known finite-line gap formula for the exponential kernel, which writes the excess inverse-matrix diversity as a sum of functions of consecutive gaps. Building on this formula, the main interval theorem proves that, for every $k\geq 2$, the unique maximizing $k$-point subset of $[0,1]$ is the equally spaced set. Thus the objective selects a uniform gap representation on the real line. A converse kernel proposition shows that, among normalized non-increasing distance kernels, requiring the corresponding adjacent-gap additive structure forces the exponential family. Further results transfer the interval theorem to ordered $\ell_1$ (L1, or Manhattan) curves by isometry: the maximizing sets are uniform in accumulated $\ell_1$ length. As a consequence, monotone biobjective Pareto fronts admit Solow--Polasky optimal finite approximations that are uniformly spaced in accumulated objective-space change, a natural representation when all parts of a continuous front should be covered. Examples, including a dense connected front and a finite disconnected ZDT3 front, illustrate how the continuous uniform-gap result appears on discrete candidate sets.
  Solow-Polasky diversity; diversity measures; finite metric magnitude; L1 distance; uniform spacing; Pareto-front approximation; multiobjective optimization; fixed-cardinality subset selection

</details>


### [126] [Achieving Directional-Stationarity from a Single Random Direction Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22045)
*Dan Greenstein,Nadav Hallak*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对约束非光滑非凸优化问题，提出一种随机方向探索步，使得任意基础优化方法都能几乎必然地收敛到方向驻点，且不破坏原方法的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在约束非光滑非凸优化中，标准方法通常只能保证弱平稳性（如Clarke驻点），难以达到更强的方向驻性（d-stationarity）。现有方法缺乏在温和正则性条件（局部Lipschitz连续、方向导数存在且连续）下获得强最优性保证的通用框架。

Method: 提出一种随机方向探索步机制：在任何基础优化方法（如DCA、prox-linear型方法）的迭代过程中，额外插入一次探索步骤——随机采样方向和步长，基于函数值比较接受候选点。该步骤不依赖基础方法的具体行为。

Result: 所有聚点几乎必然地达到d-stationarity；该探索步不破坏DCA和prox-linear型方法等基础优化方法的收敛速率；数值实验验证了探索步的效果和理论保证。

Conclusion: 随机方向探索步是一种轻量级、通用且理论保证强的策略，可无缝嵌入现有优化算法，将其收敛保证从弱平稳性提升至更强的方向驻性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of obtaining strong optimality guarantees in constrained nonsmooth nonconvex optimization under mild regularity conditions, namely local Lipschitz continuity and existence and continuity of directional derivatives. While standard methods typically ensure weak stationarity notions, achieving directional (d-)stationarity remains nontrivial. We show that a random direction exploration step is sufficient to attain d-stationarity. The proposed approach augments any base optimization method with a single exploration step that samples a direction and step size and accepts the candidate based on a function value comparison. The resulting scheme guarantees that all accumulation points are d-stationary almost surely, independently of the behavior of the underlying method. Moreover, it preserves convergence rates of the base method, as established for DCA and prox-linear-type schemes. The theoretical results are complemented by numerical experiments illustrating the effect and guarantees of the exploration step.

</details>


### [127] [Online Optimization with Unknown Time-Varying Parameters from Noisy Gradient Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22251)
*Shivanshu Tripathi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对损失函数依赖于未知时变潜参数（满足线性随机动力学）的在线优化问题，提出结合高斯-马尔可夫估计重构潜参数、工具变量估计辨识动力学、并预测未来参数以计算最小化器的算法，给出了期望跟踪误差界，并通过数值实验验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际在线优化中，损失函数常受不可观测的时变潜参数影响（如动态系统中的环境变化），而这些参数的动力学未知，现有方法难以处理此类联合估计与预测问题。

Method: 1) 利用高斯-马尔可夫估计从含噪梯度观测中重构潜参数；2) 采用工具变量估计辨识潜参数的未知线性随机动力学；3) 基于辨识的动力学预测未来参数，进而计算目标函数的未来最小化器。

Result: 推导了期望跟踪误差的理论上界，并在数值实验中验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提算法能有效处理潜参数时变且动力学未知的在线优化问题，理论保证与实验均支持其可行性。

Abstract: We study online optimization problems in which the cost function depends on latent, time-varying parameters that are unmeasurable and governed by unknown dynamics. Specifically, we consider a strongly convex cost function whose linear term evolves according to unknown linear stochastic dynamics, while the algorithm has access only to finite noisy gradient measurements. We propose a solution that uses control theoretic tools to reconstruct the latent parameters from gradient observations using a Gauss-Markov estimator, then identifies the parameter dynamics using an instrumental-variable estimator, and finally forecasts the parameters to compute the future minimizer. We provide a bound on the expected tracking error. We illustrate the effectiveness of our algorithm on a series of numerical examples.

</details>


### [128] [Incentive-Aligned Vehicle-to-Vehicle Energy Trading via Nash-Integrated Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22363)
*Yujin Lin,Yue Yang,Hao Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Nash-MADDPG方法，将纳什议价解集成到多智能体深度确定性策略梯度中，用于V2V能量交易。该方法通过纳什议价确定高效双边定价，并利用纳什引导的价格接近奖励引导智能体学习议价最优策略。30天连续运行的评估显示，相较于双拍卖机制，社会福利提升61.6%，交易量提升62.9%，公平性（Jain指数）提升40.1%。在6-100智能体规模下验证了可扩展性和定价稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的V2V能量交易方法要么依赖集中式优化存在计算限制，要么缺乏公平性保证。协调具有不同充电需求和不确定到达-离开时间表的自利电动汽车智能体仍是一个挑战。

Method: 将纳什议价解(Nash Bargaining Solution)集成到多智能体深度确定性策略梯度(MADDPG)中，形成Nash-MADDPG。纳什议价用于确定高效的双边定价，纳什引导的价格接近奖励用于引导智能体学习朝向议价最优策略。

Result: 30天连续运行中，相比于双拍卖(Double Auction)机制，社会福利提升61.6%，交易量提升62.9%，Jain公平指数提升40.1%。在6-100智能体规模、30天连续车辆轮换场景下验证了可扩展性和近纳什议价基准的稳定定价。

Conclusion: Nash-MADDPG方法能够有效协调自利的电动汽车智能体进行V2V能量交易，在提升社会福利和交易量的同时保证公平性，且具有良好的可扩展性和定价稳定性。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) energy trading enables decentralized peer-to-peer energy exchange among electric vehicles (EVs), reducing grid dependency while monetizing surplus capacity. However, coordinating self-interested EV agents with diverse charging needs and uncertain arrival-departure schedules remains challenging. Existing approaches either require centralized optimization with computational limitations or lack fairness guarantees. This paper integrates Nash Bargaining Solution into Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient, namely Nash-MADDPG, for incentive-aligned V2V energy trading. Nash bargaining determines efficient bilateral pricing, while Nash-guided price proximity rewards align agent learning toward bargaining-optimal strategies. Evaluation over 30-day continuous operation demonstrates an improvement of 61.6% in social welfare and 62.9% improvement in trading volume over Double Auction, while achieving superior fairness, such as 40.1% improvement in Jain's index. Testing across 6-100 agents over a 30-day horizon with continuous vehicle turnover confirms scalability across population size and empirically stable pricing near the Nash Bargaining benchmark.

</details>


### [129] [Global Convergence of Control-Based Lagrangian Flows for Non-Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22486)
*Simone Pirrera,Francesco Ripa,Daniele Astolfi,Sophie M. Fosson,Vito Cerone,Diego Regruto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究基于控制理论拉格朗日方法的等式约束优化连续时间动力学，针对比例积分和反馈线性化控制器诱导的动力学，利用约束流形几何结构证明非凸问题在满足特定凸性条件下的全局指数收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有收敛性结果依赖目标函数的强凸性或有界性假设，而本文旨在利用约束的几何结构来放宽这些假设条件。

Method: 采用控制理论中的比例积分控制器和反馈线性化控制器来构造动力学系统，通过分析约束流形的几何结构来证明收敛性。

Result: 对于满足约束流形上特定凸性性质的非凸问题，证明了全局指数收敛性。

Conclusion: 通过利用几何结构而非传统的强凸性或有界性假设，可以更广泛地保证等式约束优化问题的收敛性。

Abstract: This paper studies the flows of continuous-time dynamics for equality-constrained optimization based on control-theoretic Lagrangian methods. In particular, we consider dynamics induced by proportional-integral and feedback linearization controllers, which have been recently proposed as alternatives to primal-dual gradient methods. Unlike existing convergence results, which rely on strong convexity of the objective function or boundedness assumptions, we exploit the geometric structure induced by the constraints. Specifically, we show global exponential convergence for non-convex problems that satisfy a suitable convexity property when restricted to the constraint manifold.

</details>


### [130] [A conditional-gradient-based single-loop augmented Lagrangian method for inequality constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22539)
*Xiaozhou Wang,Ting Kei Pong,Zev Woodstock*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合增广拉格朗日方法与条件梯度法的算法，用于求解带有多个光滑凸不等式约束的复合凸优化问题，并证明了其收敛率与已知最优复杂度匹配，同时在一致凸集指示函数情形下实现了加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理带有多个光滑凸不等式约束的复合凸优化问题时，往往需要高效的线性最小化或acles，而经典增广拉格朗日方法在处理此类结构时效率不高。作者希望设计一种算法，既能利用条件梯度法避免投影操作，又能通过增广拉格朗日框架处理约束，从而获得最优复杂度保证。

Method: 算法在每次迭代中执行两步：(1) 对增广拉格朗日函数应用一步条件梯度法（使用开环步长或短步长两种步长规则）；(2) 采用经典AL方法的对偶变量更新，但使用递减的对偶步长。该方法不需要对约束进行投影，仅需线性最小化oracle。

Result: 在两种步长规则下，算法收敛率均达到了该类问题已知的最佳复杂度。当 $h$ 为一致凸集的指示函数时，收敛率进一步加速。

Conclusion: 本文成功将条件梯度法嵌入增广拉格朗日框架，为带有多个光滑凸约束的复合凸优化问题提供了一种无需投影、具有最优复杂度保证的算法，并在特殊结构下实现加速。

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimizing the sum of a Lipschitz differentiable convex function $f$ and a proper closed convex function $h$ that admits efficient linear minimization oracles, subject to multiple smooth convex inequality constraints. We adapt the classical augmented Lagrangian (AL) method for these problems: in each iteration, our algorithm consists of one step of the conditional gradient (CG) method applied to the AL function, followed by an update of the dual variable as in classical AL methods with a diminishing dual stepsize. We study the convergence rate of our algorithm under two standard stepsize rules for the CG method, namely, an open-loop stepsize and the short stepsize, and obtain a convergence rate that matches the best-known complexity for this class of problems. We also establish accelerated rates when $h$ is the indicator function of a uniformly convex set.

</details>


### [131] [Output regulation via input-output data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22562)
*Andrea Bisoffi,Wenjie Liu,Zhongjie Hu,Claudio De Persis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅利用含噪输入输出数据（未知外生信号干扰）为MIMO离散时间线性系统设计反馈控制器的方法，无需系统模型即可渐近消除外生信号对输出的影响，即实现了纯数据驱动的输出调节问题。控制器设计通过半定规划在辅助系统上完成，并严格论证了解在两个系统间的传递关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统输出调节问题需要已知系统模型，但在实际应用中模型往往难以获取。本文旨在仅从含噪输入输出数据出发，无需系统模型知识，直接设计控制器以实现对外生干扰的渐近抑制。

Method: 首先从MIMO离散时间线性系统的输入输出数据中收集受未知外生信号（噪声）影响的数据点；然后构建一个合适的辅助系统，在该系统上通过半定规划（SDP）设计反馈控制器；最后严格证明该控制器在辅助系统上的解可以传递到原系统，从而在原系统上实现输出调节目标。

Result: 提出了一种纯数据驱动的输出调节方法，能够仅利用受未知外生信号干扰的输入输出数据，为MIMO线性系统设计反馈控制器，渐近消除外生信号对输出的影响。

Conclusion: 本文为MIMO线性系统的输出调节问题提供了一种纯数据驱动的解决方案，通过辅助系统上的半定规划设计控制器，并建立了辅助系统与原系统之间解的关系，从而实现了无需模型知识的输出调节。

Abstract: From a multi-input-multi-output (MIMO) discrete-time linear system, we collect input-output data affected by noise in the form of an unknown exosignal and, from these data points (without knowledge of the system model), we design a feedback controller that asymptotically annihilates the effect of that exosignal on the output. This amounts to solving an output regulation problem purely from input-output data, for MIMO linear systems. The design of the controller corresponds to a semidefinite program and is pursued on a suitable auxiliary system. Such design carries over from the auxiliary system to the original one by a rigorous examination of the relation between the solutions of the two systems.

</details>


### [132] [Characterization of Normalizer of Lie Superalgebra and its Application to Control Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22694)
*Aroonima Sahoo,Kishor Chandra Pati,Tofan Kumar Khuntia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了以李超群为构型空间、包含玻色子和费米子变量的动力系统的控制问题，利用左不变向量场的李超子代数在光滑向量场李超代数中的正规化子刻画控制系统，详细研究了线性控制系统并提出了可控性判据及示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超对称理论中同时包含玻色子和费米子变量的动力系统具有重要作用，但这类系统在超群构型空间上的控制问题尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 利用左不变向量场的李超子代数在全体光滑向量场李超代数中的正规化子来刻画控制系统，对线性控制系统进行详细分析并提出可控性判据。

Result: 提出了包含玻色子和费米子变量的线性控制系统的可控性判据，并给出了具体示例。

Conclusion: 本文为李超群上同时包含玻色子和费米子变量的控制系统的可控性分析提供了理论框架和方法。

Abstract: The dynamical systems having both bosonic and fermionic variables play an important role in the theory of supersymmetry. This article addresses the control problems including both bosonic and fermionic variables on Lie supergroup as the configuration space. Here, the control systems are characterized using the normalizer of Lie subsuperalgebra of left-invariant vector fields in the Lie superalgebra of all smooth vector fields of Lie supergroup. Then, the linear control system is studied in detail and its controllability criterion is proposed along with suitable examples.

</details>


### [133] [Optimization over the intersection of manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22736)
*Yan Yang,Bin Gao,Ya-xiang Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解决两个流形交集上优化问题的几何方法，证明了清洁交集与内在横截性等价，从而得到可计算的切空间投影。该方法仅在一个流形上使用retraction，通过两个正交方向分别逼近另一个流形和降低目标函数，并在内在横截性条件下推导了收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个流形交集上的优化问题在实际应用中广泛出现，但可行区域的耦合几何特性使得优化困难。现有方法缺乏对交集正则性的统一理解和高效的几何求解策略。

Method: 1. 证明清洁交集（clean intersection）与内在横截性（intrinsic transversality）等价，从而获得可计算的切空间投影。2. 提出仅在一个流形上使用retraction的几何方法，迭代点始终停留在一个流形上，沿两个正交方向更新：一个方向用于渐近逼近另一个流形，另一个方向用于降低目标函数。

Result: 在内在横截性条件下，推导了可行性和最优性指标的收敛率，证明每个聚点都是一阶平稳点。在稀疏与低秩优化问题（球面数据拟合、双曲嵌入近似、压缩模式计算）上的数值实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 清洁交集与内在横截性的等价性为流形交集优化提供了理论支撑，所提几何方法在理论和数值上均有效，适用于稀疏与低秩优化等多个领域。

Abstract: Optimization over the intersection of two manifolds arises in a broad range of applications, but is hindered by the coupled geometry of the feasible region. In this paper, we prove that the regularities -- clean intersection and intrinsic transversality -- are equivalent, which yields a tractable projection onto the tangent space of the intersection. Therefore, we propose a geometric method that employs a retraction on only one manifold and updates the iterate along two orthogonal directions. Specifically, the iterates stay on one manifold, and the two directions are responsible for asymptotically approaching the other manifold and decreasing the objective function, respectively. Under intrinsic transversality, we derive the convergence rate for both the feasibility and optimality measures, and show that every accumulation point is first-order stationary. Numerical experiments on problems stemming from sparse and low-rank optimization, including fitting spherical data, approximating hyperbolic embeddings on real data, and computing compressed modes, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Temporal Contrastive Transformer for Financial Crime Detection: Self-Supervised Sequence Embeddings via Predictive Contrastive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21490)
*Danny Butvinik,Yonit Marcus,Nitzan Tal,Gabrielle Azoulay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出时间对比Transformer（TCT），一种用于金融交易序列表征学习的自监督框架。单独使用时嵌入特征可达AUC 0.8644，但与领域工程特征组合后无显著提升（AUC 0.9205 vs 0.9245），表明学习到的表征与已有特征存在重叠，尚未实现增量价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少金融欺诈检测中对人工特征工程的依赖，探索自监督对比学习能否自动从交易序列中捕获有意义的时序行为模式。

Method: 基于Transformer架构，采用自监督对比学习目标训练，提取金融交易序列的时序嵌入表征；下游使用梯度提升分类器进行评估。

Result: 单独嵌入AUC达0.8644，表明捕获了非平凡的时序结构；但与领域工程特征结合后AUC为0.9205，未超过纯特征基线0.9245，两者高度重叠。

Conclusion: TCT能逼近强特征工程基线的性能，表明自监督表征可近似领域特征；但当前架构尚无法提供增量价值，仍需在模型架构、训练目标和整合策略上进一步研究。

Abstract: We introduce the Temporal Contrastive Transformer (TCT), a representation learning framework designed to capture contextual temporal dynamics in sequences of financial transactions. The model is trained using a self-supervised contrastive objective to produce embeddings that encode behavioral patterns over time, with the goal of supporting downstream fraud detection tasks. We evaluate TCT in a realistic setting by using the learned embeddings as input features to a gradient boosting classifier. Experimental results show that embeddings alone achieve meaningful predictive performance (AUC 0.8644), indicating that the model captures non-trivial temporal structure. However, when combined with domain-engineered features, no measurable improvement is observed over the baseline (AUC 0.9205 vs. 0.9245), suggesting that the learned representations largely overlap with existing feature abstractions. These findings position TCT as a promising representation learning approach that captures relevant behavioral signal, while highlighting the challenges of achieving additive value over strong domain features. The results reflect an intermediate stage in the development of temporal representation learning for financial crime detection and motivate further research on model architecture, training objectives, and integration strategies. At this early stage, achieving performance comparable to a strong feature-engineered baseline is itself a meaningful outcome, indicating that learned representations approximate domain-specific features without manual engineering. While not yet production-ready, these results point to a promising direction for reducing reliance on feature engineering in financial crime detection.

</details>


### [135] [Teaching Language Models to Forecast Research Success Through Comparative Idea Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21491)
*Srujan P Mule,Aniketh Garikaparthi,Manasi Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型能否在实验前预测研究想法的实证成功。通过构建基于PapersWithCode的11,488对想法数据集，发现小模型经SFT可达到77.1%的预测准确率，优于GPT-5（61.1%）；通过RLVR框架进一步实现71.35%准确率并生成可解释推理路径。结果表明计算高效的小模型可作为客观验证器，为自主科学发现提供可扩展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型自动化生成研究假设，评估和筛选大量AI生成的想法成为新的瓶颈。本文旨在探索是否可以用语言模型在实验前预测研究想法的实证成功，以解决自动化科学发现中的验证瓶颈问题。

Method: 构建基于PapersWithCode的11,488对研究想法数据集（含客观实验结果）；采用SFT方法微调8B小模型；提出RLVR（基于可验证奖励的强化学习）框架将评估建模为推理任务，训练模型发现潜在推理路径。

Result: 原始8B模型仅30%准确率；SFT后达到77.1%，超越GPT-5的61.1%；RLVR实现71.35%准确率并生成可解释理由；通过消融实验和分布外测试验证了模型对表面启发式的鲁棒性以及跨域迁移能力。

Conclusion: 计算高效的小语言模型可以充当有效的客观验证器，为自主科学发现中想法筛选提供可扩展方案，在实证预测任务上甚至优于大模型。

Abstract: As language models accelerate scientific research by automating hypothesis generation and implementation, a new bottleneck emerges: evaluating and filtering hundreds of AI-generated ideas without exhaustive experimentation. We ask whether LMs can learn to forecast the empirical success of research ideas before any experiments are run. We study comparative empirical forecasting: given a benchmark-specific research goal and two candidate ideas, predict which will achieve better benchmark performance. We construct a dataset of 11,488 idea pairs grounded in objective outcomes from PapersWithCode. While off-the-shelf 8B-parameter models struggle (30% acc.), SFT dramatically boosts performance to 77.1%, outperforming GPT-5 (61.1%). By framing evaluation as a reasoning task via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), we train models to discover latent reasoning paths, achieving 71.35% acc. with interpretable justifications. Through additional ablations and out-of-distribution tests, we show robustness to surface-level heuristics and transfer to both a cross-domain time-split test set and an independently constructed test set. Our results demonstrate that compute-efficient small language models can serve as effective, objective verifiers, offering a scalable path for autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [136] [Don't Collapse Your Features: Why CenterLoss Hurts OOD Detection and Multi-Scale Mahalanobis Wins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21493)
*Rahul D Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GOEN是一种简单高效的OOD检测方法，通过多尺度特征、L2归一化、马氏距离和校准头组合实现。关键发现：CenterLoss虽然提升分类精度但会损害OOD检测性能，最佳变体GOEN-NoCenterLoss在CIFAR-10上达到0.9483 AUROC，超越深度集成等基线，训练仅需不到20分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法依赖为分类精度优化的特征表示，忽视了认知不确定性的独特需求。作者旨在设计一种简单高效的OOD检测方法，并探究分类几何与OOD检测性能之间的关系。

Method: GOEN管道包含四个组件：(1)多尺度特征提取；(2)L2归一化；(3)马氏距离度量；(4)使用真实困难OOD样本训练的校准头。通过系统消融实验，对比了使用与不使用CenterLoss的效果。

Result: GOEN-NoCenterLoss在CIFAR-10上平均OOD AUROC达0.9483，显著优于深度集成(0.8827)、KNN(0.8967)和ODIN(0.8870)。CenterLoss将AUROC从0.9483降至0.9366，尽管提升分类精度。单GPU训练时间少于20分钟。

Conclusion: 更好的分类几何（如通过CenterLoss实现的紧致特征聚类）并不自动带来更好的认知不确定性估计。过度紧致的特征聚类会压缩类间边界、扭曲协方差结构，反而损害OOD检测性能。

Abstract: The ability to detect out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs is fundamental to safe deployment of machine learning systems. Yet, current methods often rely on feature representations that are optimised solely for classification accuracy, neglecting the distinct requirements of epistemic uncertainty. We introduce GOEN (Geometry-Optimised Epistemic Network), a simple pipeline that combines multi-scale features, L2 normalisation, Mahalanobis distance, and a calibration head trained with real hard OOD examples. Through systematic ablation we uncover a counter-intuitive finding: CenterLoss, a popular regulariser for feature compactness, significantly degrades OOD detection performance, reducing average OOD AUROC from 0.9483 to 0.9366 despite improving classification accuracy. The best variant, GOEN-NoCenterLoss, achieves an average OOD AUROC of 0.9483, surpassing all baselines including deep ensembles (0.8827), KNN (0.8967), and ODIN (0.8870) on CIFAR-10 benchmarks, while maintaining competitive in-distribution accuracy. Our results challenge the prevailing assumption that better classification geometry automatically leads to better epistemic uncertainty. Instead, we show that overly tight feature clusters compress inter-class margins and distort the covariance structure needed for effective OOD detection. GOEN is efficient, training in under 20 minutes on a single GPU, and provides a practical blueprint for building AI systems that reliably recognise their own limitations.

</details>


### [137] [Double descent for least-squares interpolation on contaminated data: A simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21494)
*Tino Werner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了过参数化线性回归模型中，在训练数据被污染（存在异常值）的情况下，是否会出现“双下降”现象。结果显示，高度非鲁棒的最小二乘插值估计器在高度过参数化时确实展现出双下降现象，其泛化性能甚至超越了鲁棒替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计理论认为过参数化模型容易过拟合，但实际中过参数化模型却表现优异。双下降现象的发现开启了新的研究方向。同时，鲁棒统计关注被污染数据下的估计问题，但两者结合的研究尚不充分。本文旨在探究在存在数据污染（异常值）的线性回归场景中，双下降现象是否仍然存在。

Method: 在带污染的线性回归训练数据设定下，比较高度非鲁棒的最小二乘插值估计器与多种鲁棒替代方法的性能表现，观察随模型复杂度变化的泛化误差趋势。

Result: 高度过参数化确实能够引发双下降现象，使得最小二乘插值估计器获得非常好的泛化性能，甚至超越了鲁棒替代方法。

Conclusion: 在存在数据污染的情况下，过参数化仍能带来双下降效应，且非鲁棒的最小二乘估计器在高度过参数化区域表现优于鲁棒方法，这一发现挑战了传统鲁棒统计的直觉。

Abstract: Overparametrized models can exhibit an excellent generalization performance, although they should be prone to overfitting according to classical statistical theory. The discovery of the "double descent", indicating that the generalization error decreases after a certain model complexity has been reached, opened a new line of research. Robust statistics considers statistical estimation on contaminated data, which, due to assumptions that do not hold on real data, let data points appear as outliers w.r.t. the assumed "ideal" distribution, potentially severely distorting any classical estimator. We address the question whether a double descent phenomenon can be observed in a linear regression setting with contaminated training data. We compare the performance of the highly non-robust least-squares interpolation estimator with several robust alternatives. It turns out that large overparametrization indeed allows for a double descent phenomenon, resulting in a very good generalization performance of the least-squares interpolator, surpassing that of the robust alternatives.

</details>


### [138] [HealthCraft: A Reinforcement Learning Safety Environment for Emergency Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21496)
*Brandon Dent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HealthCraft是首个基于FHIR R4的急诊医学强化学习环境，包含24个MCP工具、195个任务和2,255个二元评估标准（其中515个为安全关键）。测试表明，前沿模型在多步骤急诊工作流中表现极差（Pass@1接近0%），安全失败率高达27.5%-34.0%，且基础设施错误会显著改变模型排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态医学QA基准无法捕捉急诊医学中的关键失败模式（如轨迹级安全崩溃、工具误用、临床压力下的妥协），而前沿语言模型正以前所未有的速度部署到临床工作流中，急需能评估轨迹级安全性的基础设施。

Method: 基于Corecraft改造，构建FHIR R4世界状态（14种实体类型、3,987个种子实体），暴露24个MCP工具；设计双层评分体系（安全关键标准违规则奖励归零）；发布195个任务（6大类）及2,255个二元标准（515个安全关键）；使用确定性LLM-judge覆盖层控制评估者噪声。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6 Pass@1为24.8%，GPT-5.4为12.6%；安全失败率分别为27.5%和34.0%。多步骤工作流中性能崩溃至接近零（Claude 1.0%，GPT-5.4 0.0%）。v2到v8修复的6个基础设施错误改变了模型排名顺序。

Conclusion: 现有前沿模型在急诊医学多步骤工作流中远未达到安全部署标准；基础设施保真度直接影响评估结果；该环境可作为安全对齐训练和评估的基础设施，但奖励信号不宜直接用于训练（存在可游戏化风险）。

Abstract: Frontier language models are being deployed into clinical workflows faster than the infrastructure to evaluate them safely. Static medical-QA benchmarks miss the failure modes that matter in emergency medicine: trajectory-level safety collapse, tool misuse, and capitulation under sustained clinical pressure. We present HealthCraft, the first public reinforcement-learning environment that rewards trajectory-level safety under realistic emergency-medicine conditions, adapted from Corecraft. It is built on a FHIR R4 world state with 14 entity types and 3,987 seed entities, exposes 24 MCP tools, and defines a dual-layer rubric that zeroes reward whenever any safety-critical criterion is violated. We release 195 tasks across six categories, graded against 2,255 binary criteria (515 safety-critical); a post-hoc 10-task negative-class slate extends this to 205 tasks and 2,337 criteria. V8 results on two frontier models show Claude Opus 4.6 at Pass@1 24.8% [21.5-28.4] and GPT-5.4 at 12.6% [10.2-15.6], with safety-failure rates of 27.5% and 34.0%. On multi-step workflows - the closest proxy to real emergency care - performance collapses to near zero (Claude 1.0%, GPT-5.4 0.0%) despite partial competence on individual steps. Six infrastructure bugs fixed between pilots v2 and v8 re-ordered which model "looks stronger," evidence that infrastructure fidelity is part of the measurement. A deterministic LLM-judge overlay bounds evaluator noise, and a 60-run negative-class smoke pilot shows the reward signal is not drop-in training-safe: restraint criteria pass at 0.929 prevalence, a gameability an eval harness can tolerate but a training reward cannot. We scaffold coupling to a Megatron+SGLang+GRPO loop per Corecraft Section 5.2 and leave training-reward ablations as future work. Environment, tasks, rubrics, and harness are released under Apache 2.0.

</details>


### [139] [Harnesses for Inference-Time Alignment over Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21516)
*Boyuan Wang,Bochao Li,Minghan Wang,Yuxin Tao,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从推理时轨迹对齐的角度研究LLM Agent的Harness设计，将其分解为任务分解和引导执行两个机制，揭示了过分解、过度剪枝和幻觉执行等失效模式，并发现部分指定的Harness（仅指定初始步骤）可能比完整结构化工作流更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harness技术虽能提升LLM Agent长期性能，但更复杂的Harness并非总是更好——增加分解或引导有时能改善执行，有时反而降低任务成功率。作者希望从理论角度量化这种非线性关系，并揭示其背后的失效机制。

Method: 将Harness设计分解为任务分解（结构化为子目标）和引导执行（重塑局部动作分布）两个机制；通过推理时轨迹对齐的理论框架量化工作流粒度、重试预算和引导引起的动作重加权对性能极限的影响；在受控合成实验和真实终端Agent基准上验证预测。

Result: 揭示了三种失效模式：过分解（over-decomposition）、过度剪枝（over-pruning）和幻觉执行（hallucinated execution）；发现部分Harness（仅指定初始步骤，后续由Agent自主执行）比完整结构化工作流具有更高的通过率。

Conclusion: Harness设计不应一味追求更细粒度的分解和更强的引导；适当的"部分指定"策略——让Agent在后续步骤中自主发挥——反而可能更优。这为实际部署中的Harness设计提供了理论指导和实用建议。

Abstract: Harness engineering has emerged as an important inference-time technique for large language model (LLM) agents, aiming to improve long-term performance through task decomposition and guided execution. However, more elaborate harnesses are not uniformly better: increasing decomposition or guidance can sometimes improve execution, but can also reduce final task success. We study harness design through the lens of inference-time trajectory alignment. This perspective separates harness into two mechanisms: task decomposition, which structures a task into sub-goals, and guided execution, which reshapes local action distributions during execution. This decomposition allows us to quantify how workflow granularity, retry budgets, and guidance-induced action reweighting shape the performance limits of harness design. It further reveals concrete failure modes, including over-decomposition, over-pruning, and hallucinated execution. We validate these predictions through controlled synthetic experiments and real terminal agent benchmarks. Inspired by the theory, we further show that effective harnesses can be partial: specifying only the initial steps and leaving the remaining execution to agent can achieve higher pass rate than fully structured workflows.

</details>


### [140] [A Reproducible Log-Driven AutoML Framework for Interpretable Pipeline Optimization in Healthcare Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21528)
*Rui Huang,Lican Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出yvsoucom-iterkit，一个确定性、日志驱动的自动化机器学习框架，将流水线优化编码为可追溯的日志实体。在Pima印第安人糖尿病和中风数据集上评估超过18,000种流水线配置，揭示了结构化且部分冗余的搜索空间，其中性能由少数关键组件主导。集成模型取得优异性能（Pima上Weighted-F1 0.89，Macro-F1 0.88；Stroke上Weighted-F1 0.94），且跨种子分析表明集成模型比SVM具有更低的变异性（0.023-0.026）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的疾病风险预测因异质性特征、有限样本和严重类别不平衡而面临挑战。现有方法缺乏可复现性和对组件贡献、交互作用的系统分析能力。

Method: 提出了yvsoucom-iterkit框架，一种确定性、日志驱动的自动化机器学习方法。将每条流水线编码为可追溯的日志实体，支持组件归因、交互、相似性和跨种子鲁棒性分析。采用Random Forest重要性分析、组件相似性分析（RMS距离）和集成模型策略。

Result: 在Pima数据集上，关键驱动因素为数据增强（0.454）、模型选择（0.198）和类别不平衡处理（0.101）；Stroke数据集上类别不平衡处理主导（0.406）。发现大量组件冗余（如biMax-biMean特征选择RMS距离0.0252，mixup与无增强距离0.0279）。集成模型在Pima上Weighted-F1 0.89/Macro-F1 0.88，Stroke上Weighted-F1 0.94/Macro-F1 0.67。集成模型跨种子变异性0.023-0.026，低于SVM。

Conclusion: 有效的AutoML优化可聚焦于少数高影响组件；集成方法在性能和鲁棒性之间取得良好平衡；搜索空间存在结构化冗余，可通过降低冗余提高效率。性能-鲁棒性之间存在权衡关系。

Abstract: Accurate and reproducible disease risk prediction remains challenging due to heterogeneous features, limited samples, and severe class imbalance. This study introduces yvsoucom-iterkit, a deterministic and log-driven automated machine learning framework that formulates pipeline optimization as a fully reproducible, configuration-level system. Each pipeline is encoded as a traceable log entity, enabling analysis of component attribution, interactions, similarity, and cross-seed robustness. Experiments on the Pima Indians Diabetes and Stroke datasets across more than 18,000 pipeline configurations reveal a structured and partially redundant search space, where performance is governed by a small subset of interacting components. Random Forest importance analysis identifies augmentation (0.454), model choice (0.198), and imbalance handling (0.101) as key drivers on Pima, while imbalance handling dominates Stroke (0.406). Component similarity analysis shows strong redundancy, with feature selection variants (biMax-biMean) exhibiting low RMS distance (0.0252), mixup closely matching no augmentation (0.0279), and TomekLinks aligning with no imbalance handling (0.0325), whereas Gaussian noise shows greater divergence from no augmentation (0.10). The framework achieves strong and stable performance using ensemble models (Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88 on Pima; Weighted-F1 0.94 on Stroke), while Macro-F1 remains lower on Stroke (0.67) due to class imbalance. Cross-seed analysis reveals a performance-robustness trade-off, with ensembles showing lower variability (0.023-0.026) than SVM. These results indicate that effective AutoML optimization can focus on a reduced set of high-impact components.

</details>


### [141] [DualOptim+: Bridging Shared and Decoupled Optimizer States for Better Machine Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21539)
*Xuyang Zhong,Qizhang Li,Yiwen Guo,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualOptim+ 是一种针对大语言模型机器遗忘的优化框架，通过引入基础状态和增量状态来平衡遗忘与保留目标，并提供了8位量化变体以减少内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型的机器遗忘任务中，遗忘目标与保留目标之间存在梯度方向冲突，现有方法难以在这两者之间取得良好平衡，亟需一种能够自适应协调这两种目标的优化框架。

Method: 提出DualOptim+框架，引入基础状态（base state）捕捉遗忘和保留目标的共享表示，以及增量状态（delta states）保留目标特定的残差信息。优化器根据遗忘梯度和保留梯度之间的方向冲突，自适应地在共享和解耦状态之间进行桥接。同时提出DualOptim+ 8bit量化变体降低内存开销。

Result: 在虚构/真实世界遗忘、安全对齐和多任务学习等任务上，DualOptim+ consistently achieves a superior trade-off between different objectives，量化版本在不牺牲性能的前提下降低了内存开销。

Conclusion: DualOptim+ 通过自适应桥接共享与解耦状态，有效平衡了机器遗忘中遗忘与保留目标之间的冲突，且量化变体进一步提升了实用性。

Abstract: We propose DualOptim+, a novel optimization framework for improving machine unlearning in large language models. It introduces a base state to capture common representations shared by forgetting and retaining objectives and delta states to preserve objective-specific residuals. This architecture allows the optimizer to adaptively bridge shared and decoupled states based on the directional conflict between forgetting and retaining gradients. We further introduce DualOptim+ 8bit, a quantized variant that reduces memory overhead without compromising performance. Extensive experiments across fictitious and real-world unlearning, safety alignment, and multi-task learning tasks demonstrate that DualOptim+ consistently achieves a superior trade-off between different objectives. Codes are available at https://github.com/CityU-MLO/DualOptimPlus.

</details>


### [142] [From Sequential Nodes to GPU Batches: Parallel Branch and Bound for Optimal $k$-Sparse GLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22188)
*Jiachang Liu,Andrea Lodi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种CPU-GPU混合框架，通过批量处理分支定界节点和轻量级自定义内核，将GPU加速应用于带基数约束的广义线性模型的离散优化，实现了一到两个数量级的加速和零最优间隙，并能扩展到收集完整Rashomon集以支持下游统计分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU显著加速了连续优化的一阶方法，但离散变量、组合结构和非线性目标（如基数约束广义线性模型的最优解认证）的优化问题未受益于此，主要挑战包括分支定界中异构节点的顺序处理以及CPU与GPU间的频繁数据移动。

Method: 提出一种简单、通用、模块化的CPU-GPU框架，通过批量处理GPU上的多个分支定界节点，围绕一小套GPU高效例程构建，使用填充(padding)和轻量级自定义内核处理不规则的节点数据结构。

Result: 在具有挑战性的实例上实现一到两个数量级的加速和零最优间隙。框架还可扩展收集整个Rashomon集，支持变量重要性分析和基于用户自定义指标（如AUC）的模型选择等下游统计分析。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效将GPU加速引入离散组合优化场景，显著提升求解效率且保持最优性，并扩展到统计学习中的可解释性分析。

Abstract: GPUs have significantly accelerated first-order methods for large-scale optimization, especially in continuous optimization. However, this success has not transferred cleanly to problems with discrete variables, combinatorial structure, and nonlinear objectives, such as certifying optimal solutions for cardinality-constrained generalized linear models. Major challenges include the sequential processing of heterogeneous nodes in branch and bound (BnB) and frequent data movement between the CPU and GPU. We propose a simple, generic, and modular CPU--GPU framework that processes multiple BnB nodes in batches on GPUs. The framework is built around a small set of GPU-efficient routines and uses padding together with lightweight custom kernels to handle irregular node data structures. Experiments show one to two orders of magnitude speedups and zero optimality gap on challenging instances. The framework can also be extended to collect the entire Rashomon set, enabling downstream statistical analysis such as variable-importance analysis and model selection under secondary user-specific measures (e.g., AUC in classification).

</details>


### [143] [A note on convergence of Wasserstein policy optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22622)
*David Šiška,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在熵正则化马尔可夫决策过程框架下，Wasserstein策略优化（WPO）算法能够实现线性收敛。通过利用平均场分析中的对数Sobolev不等式，在假设梯度流方程存在足够规则解的条件下，证明了单调能量耗散和局部对数Sobolev不等式，从而推导出值函数线性收敛到全局最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管WPO在连续动作空间的随机策略优化中取得了经验成功，但其在连续状态和动作空间环境下的理论收敛性质尚未得到充分建立，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 在熵正则化MDP框架下分析WPO；利用平均场理论中的对数Sobolev不等式推导梯度流的收敛性；假设梯度流方程存在足够规则解，证明单调能量耗散和局部对数Sobolev不等式成立。

Result: 证明了在熵正则化MDP框架下WPO具有线性收敛速率，值函数线性收敛到全局最优。

Conclusion: WPO在连续状态和动作空间的熵正则化MDP中具有线性收敛保证，为该算法的理论基础提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: Wasserstein Policy Optimization (WPO) is a recently proposed reinforcement learning algorithm that leverages Wasserstein gradient flows to optimize stochastic policies in continuous action spaces. Despite its empirical success, the theoretical convergence properties of WPO in environments with continuous state and action spaces have yet to be fully established. In this note, we argue that WPO within the framework of entropy-regularised Markov Decision Processes converges linearly. This is done by leveraging recent advances in mean-field analysis for convergence of gradient flows using log-Sobole inequalities. Assuming existence of sufficiently regular solution to the gradient flow equation we demonstrate monotonic energy dissipation along the flow and establish a local log-Sobolev inequality. Ultimately, these properties allow us to argue that the value function should converge linearly to the global optimum.

</details>


### [144] [Provable Joint Decontamination for Benchmarking Multiple Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21543)
*Zhenlong Liu,Hao Zeng,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联合包络共形选择（JECS）方法，通过共形推断实现多模型基准去污的全局污染率（GCR）控制，在保持理论保证的同时比最大p值基线方法具有更高统计功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准数据污染检测方法缺乏理论保证，或仅能针对单模型提供可控假阳性率，无法支持多模型间的公平比较。需要一种能够跨多个模型联合控制全局污染率的方法。

Method: JECS为每个模型计算共形p值，按数据点取最大值聚合；利用右尾数据重建最大p值零分布的保守包络；通过自适应Benjamini-Hochberg（BH）过程对包络缩放后的值进行筛选，实现全局污染率控制。

Result: 在多个模型和基准上的实验表明，JECS相比最大p值基线方法具有更高的统计功效（power），同时能持续维持目标全局污染率控制。

Conclusion: JECS为多模型基准去污提供了首个具有理论保证的联合选择框架，有效解决了跨模型公平比较中的数据污染问题。

Abstract: Benchmark data contamination has become a central challenge in LLM evaluation: when evaluation examples appear in the training data of one or more audited models, reported performance can be inflated and cross-model comparisons become unreliable. A broad line of training-data detection work designs scores to quantify how strongly a model memorizes a given data point, but these score-based methods lack theoretical guarantees. Recent conformal approaches provide provable false-identification control for a single model; however, applying them separately to each model can produce model-specific benchmarks, undermining fair comparison across models. In this work, we formalize multi-model benchmark decontamination as a joint selection problem and propose Joint Envelope Conformal Selection (JECS), a conformal procedure that enables global contamination rate (GCR) control under stated assumptions. Specifically, JECS computes per-model conformal p-values, aggregates them by the per-item maximum, and reconstructs a conservative envelope of the max-p null distribution from right-tail observations above a data-driven threshold. By applying the adaptive Benjamini-Hochberg (BH) procedure to the envelope-rescaled values, we select a benchmark with provable GCR control. Extensive experiments across various models and benchmarks demonstrate that JECS achieves higher power than the max-p baseline while consistently maintaining the target GCR control.

</details>


### [145] [Tabular foundation models for robust calibration of near-infrared chemical sensing data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21544)
*Robin Reiter,Denis Cornet,Fabien Michel,Lauriane Rouan,Gregory Beurier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了表格基础模型TabPFN在近红外光谱化学传感中的应用潜力，在66个NIR数据集上对比了多种预处理和建模策略，发现TabPFN在回归和分类任务中均取得优异表现，尤其在中小型校准集上可 complement 传统化学计量学方法，但在光谱异常值和外推样本上优势减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NIR光谱传感器在实际部署中依赖校准模型，但现有方法面临高维共线光谱、样本量有限、预处理依赖、光谱异常值和域外推等挑战。本文旨在探索表格基础模型（如TabPFN）是否能作为一种新的校准策略来应对上述问题。

Method: 在66个NIR数据集（54个回归+12个分类任务）上，将TabPFN与PLS/PLS-DA、Ridge、CatBoost和1D-CNN进行对比，采用统一的验证框架（预处理和模型选择仅在标定数据上进行），评估直接推理和预处理优化推理两种模式。

Result: 回归任务中，预处理优化的TabPFN取得最佳平均排名，显著优于PLS、CatBoost、原始光谱TabPFN和CNN-1D，与Ridge统计相当；分类任务中，直接应用于原始光谱的TabPFN取得最佳平均排名。在光谱异常值和外推样本上，TabPFN的优势减弱，传统化学计量学模型仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型可补充NIR化学传感的既有化学计量学工作流，尤其适用于中小型校准场景，但需进一步发展光谱特异性先验知识和不确定性感知部署策略。

Abstract: Near-infrared spectroscopy is increasingly used as a rapid, non-destructive chemical sensing technology for the analysis of food, pharmaceutical, biological, and environmental samples. However, the practical deployment of NIR sensors still depends on calibration models able to handle high-dimensional, collinear spectra, limited sample sizes, preprocessing dependence, spectral outliers, and extrapolation beyond the calibration domain. Here, we evaluate whether tabular foundation models can provide a new calibration strategy for NIR chemical sensing. We benchmark TabPFN on 66 NIR datasets covering 54 regression and 12 classification tasks, and compare direct inference on raw spectra with preprocessing-optimized inference against PLS/PLS-DA, Ridge, Catboost, and one-dimensional convolutional neural networks. The study uses a unified validation framework in which preprocessing and model selection are performed exclusively on calibration data before external test evaluation. In regression, preprocessing-optimized TabPFN achieves the best overall average rank and significantly outperforms PLS, CatBoost, TabPFN on raw spectra, and CNN-1D, while remaining statistically comparable to Ridge. In classification, TabPFN applied directly to raw spectra provides the best average rank, with performance close to the optimized variant. Robustness analyses show that TabPFN provides strong average predictive performance but that its advantage decreases on spectral outliers and extrapolated samples, where classical chemometric models remain competitive. These results suggest that tabular foundation models can complement established chemometric workflows for NIR chemical sensing, especially in small- to medium-sized calibration settings, while highlighting the need for spectroscopy-specific priors and uncertainty-aware deployment strategies.

</details>


### [146] [Expectation Consistency Loss: Rethink Confidence Calibration under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21552)
*Jinzong Dong,Zhaohui Jiang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于期望一致性条件（Expectation Consistency Condition）的置信度校准方法，用于解决协变量偏移下的模型校准问题，并设计了兼容多种校准形式的期望一致性损失（ECL），理论证明了其样本复杂度与ECE一致，并在模拟和真实数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法大多假设训练集和测试集独立同分布，在协变量偏移下校准效果受限；而现有协变量偏移校准方法存在密度比估计不稳定、仅适用于特定校准形式等局限。

Method: 首先推导了协变量偏移下置信度校准的充要条件——期望一致性条件；基于该条件提出无监督域适应损失ECL，兼容典范校准、逐类校准和顶部标签校准；理论分析了ECL的样本复杂度，并设计了基于小批量的可训练方案。

Result: 在模拟和真实协变量偏移数据集上验证了ECL方法的有效性，能够稳定地校准目标域置信度。

Conclusion: 协变量偏移不一定导致置信度失校准，期望一致性条件提供了比全局分布对齐更弱的校准条件；ECL损失在理论和实践上均能有效解决协变量偏移下的置信度校准问题。

Abstract: Confidence calibration for classification models is vital in safety-critical decision-making scenarios and has received extensive attention. General confidence calibration methods assume training and test data are independent and identically distributed, limiting their effectiveness under covariate shifts. Previous calibration methods under covariate shift struggle with class-wise or canonical calibrations and often rely on unstable importance weighting when density ratios are large or unbounded. Given the above limitations, this paper rethinks confidence calibration under covariate shifts. First, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for confidence calibration under covariate shifts, named Expectation consistency condition, which reveals covariate shifts do not necessarily lead to uncalibrated confidence and provides a weaker condition for confidence calibration than global covariate distribution alignment. Then, utilizing Expectation consistency condition, this paper proposes an unsupervised domain adaptation loss to calibrate confidence of the target domain, named Expectation consistency loss (ECL), which is compatible with canonical calibration, class-wise calibration, and top-label calibration. Third, we prove that computing ECL loss has the same sample complexity as Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and provide a theoretically grounded mini-batch trainable scheme for ECL loss. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our method on both simulated and real-world covariate shift datasets.

</details>
